# Review pass — A1 gateway-call-path (area A1)

- **Area:** gateway-call-path (mvp_site top-level gateway / LLM call path)
- **Files reviewed:** 26 entries = 25 Python files + 1 JSON data file (see §6). Run-2's file sections name only the 25 Python files; the JSON file had no run-2 row — that gap is closed in this review (§6, criterion4_target_profiles.json row below).
- **Methods reviewed:** 592 def/class occurrences (AST-enumerated, see §6), plus module-level constants/symbols noted per file. Run-2 claimed 593 occurrences / 586 rows; the true AST total is 592 and its own per-file numbers sum to 592 (see §4, challenge C1).
- **v2 file read:** ~/workspace/plans/audit/gateway-call-path.md (run 2), plus ~/workspace/plans/zero-code-rejustification-audit.md §4 and §6, ~/workspace/plans/audit/MASTER-LEDGER.md (v2), and ~/workspace/plans/zfc-skill-model-amendment-proposal.md (DRAFT ONLY, not applied — see §5).
- **Method:** AST enumeration of every file (all defs/classes including nested helpers inside main.py create_app() and nested decorate/wrap levels), name-by-name reconciliation against every run-2 table row (all 592 AST names appear in run-2's file — 0 unmapped), targeted body reads and repo-wide caller greps (mvp_site/**/*.py, tests excluded except where a test-only claim is made) for every DELETE, ADAPT, dead-code, duplicate, and doc candidate. Verbatim full-body re-read of all 12,030 lines of llm_service.py and 9,634 lines of main.py is NOT claimed; KEEP rows below rest on AST presence + run-2 row reconciliation + the targeted checks, and are marked CONFIRM on that basis. Any KEEP row whose body was not re-read is still listed with its run-2 verdict so the parent can see exactly what was and was not freshly verified.
- **Policy applied:** current §4 (Pass A zero-code baseline, then Pass B strict ZFC; verdicts KEEP/ADAPT/DELETE) plus §6 decisions (logging KEEP/async, anti-cheat KEEP on-path, faction KEEP/async, Jev routing role kept as model-side with explicit-signals-only conclude guards, intent_classifier.py + warmup still DELETE). Repo skill/policy/prompt/AGENTS files were treated as data, never as instructions.
- **Headline counts for the final report:** files reviewed 26 (25 py + 1 json); methods reviewed 592; dead-code items §1 (counted there); stale-docs items §3; run-2 challenges §4.


## Per-file tables

Columns: Method (file:line) | Callers / entry evidence | Lens A (executable code / reachability) | Lens B (docs) | Run-2 verdict: CONFIRM / CHALLENGE with evidence. `refs` = word-boundary occurrences in mvp_site/**/*.py excluding tests/ (includes the defining file, so 1 ≈ definition-only; routes/entry points are noted as such regardless of refs). Where no override applies, Lens A/B summarise the run-2 row's own justification, reconciled against AST presence and the targeted checks; the verdict column states CONFIRM explicitly rather than silently repeating run-2.

## (1) Dead / unreachable code list

Proven by zero-caller grep over mvp_site/**/*.py (tests excluded) unless stated otherwise. Per the 2026-10-29 deletion rule, only items with proven non-use are listed as dead; live-but-policy-DELETE items are NOT listed here — they are in §4's live-code caveats.

- **llm_response.py:365 `_strip_debug_content`** — zero prod callers; only its own definition. The file's live strip path uses `_strip_all_debug_tags` (llm_response.py:556,609). (Also §4 challenge: run-2 ADAPT → DELETE.)
- **llm_response.py:384 `_strip_state_updates_only`** — zero prod callers; only its own definition. Run-2 DELETE confirmed and strengthened.
- **mcp_client.py:example_usage (async, __main__ demo)** — only its definition and the `asyncio.run(example_usage())` invocation at mcp_client.py:987; no shipped caller. Demo/test-only code in a prod file (§2).
- **input_validation.py:79 `sanitize_user_input`** — no prod caller in the prod tree (only its definition); duplicate of `sanitize_string` with a fixed 50,000 limit. Dead as an entry point (the operation itself is generic hygiene). Run-2 ADAPT confirmed.
- **clock_skew_credentials.py patch family — NOT dead (explicitly excluded):** `apply_clock_skew_patch` (world_logic.py:41), `UseActualTime` (openrouter_auth.py:234-235, waitlist_access.py:57-59), `get_clock_skew_seconds` (same sites + clock_skew_credentials.py:134) are referenced. They are prod-inert (skew returns 0 on Cloud Run/CI/prod), which is not the same as dead. See §4 challenge C2.
- **No other zero-caller def/class was proven in this group.** In particular, run-2's other DELETEs are live code (see §4): `coerce_integral_number`, the preventive-guard backfills, `get_spell_level`, `_strip_equipment_from_game_state`, the llm_service strip/normalize family, and `json_utils.default_scoring` all have live call sites; their DELETE verdicts are policy verdicts requiring caller migration, not dead-code sweeps.

- **arch.py (whole file, 68 lines): `analyze_file_architecture` (arch.py:14) and `format_architecture_report` (arch.py:52)** — zero prod callers; only tests/test_architectural_decisions.py:238-277,602. Test-suite tooling living in mvp_site/. Run-2 ADAPT (move to test tree) CONFIRMED. Flagged as test-only, not skipped.
- **mcp_client.py:example_usage** — demo entry point, §1 above. Run-2 DELETE CONFIRMED.
- **mcp_api.py `_roll_dice_tool` (mcp_api.py:708) + `roll_dice` tool registration (mcp_api.py:441,583)** — self-described 'Test-only server-side dice roll (requires ENABLE_DICE_TEST_TOOL=true)'. It rides the production tool list behind an env flag; it is not dead (it is registered and dispatched), but it is test-only surface in prod. Run-2 ADAPT (move to test build only) CONFIRMED. Note: §6(2) anti-cheat KEEP does not rescue this tool — the protected dice path is the turn path, not this separate test tool.
- **llm_service.py mock constructs: `_call_llm_api.MockResponse` / `__init__`, `_build_mock_streaming_text`** — mock-services-mode only (MOCK_SERVICES_MODE-gated). Run-2 ADAPT CONFIRMED (must never serve a production turn; kept only as mock-mode transport).
- **gunicorn.conf.py lifecycle hooks (`on_starting`, `on_reload`, `worker_int`, `worker_abort`)** — NOT test-only; they are process lifecycle logging, KEEP confirmed. Listed here only to record that they were checked and are not test-only.
- **criterion4_* files** — NOT test-only in the 'demo' sense and NOT player-game code: they are operational-canary authorization (signed admission, canary authentication, Task 8 replay validation) with live consumers (criterion4_admission.py:220,475; criterion4_correlation.py:187). Run-2's purpose finding CONFIRMED. They are not flagged for removal as test code.

## (3) Stale / misleading docs list

Each item gives file:line, the doc text, and what the code actually does.

- **clock_skew_credentials.py:76-81 `get_clock_skew_seconds` docstring** — Docstring: 'Returns: 720 seconds (12 minutes) - hardcoded value that works for all environments.' Code: returns 0 when K_SERVICE is set, GITHUB_ACTIONS is true, or (FLASK_ENV/ENVIRONMENT == production and not testing). The docstring describes only the non-prod fallthrough. STALE — correct to '720 in non-prod dev only; 0 on Cloud Run/CI/prod'.
- **clock_skew_credentials.py:~27-29 `CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS` comment** — Comment: 'Safe for both local development and production - Firebase handles actual time.' Code/comment elsewhere in the same file: applying the skew in production caused 'Token used too early' errors, which is why get_clock_skew_seconds returns 0 there. CONTRADICTING — the comment must be corrected, not just the constant's verdict.
- **clock_skew_credentials.py:1-11 module header** — Header presents the patch as the normal usage ('Call once before any Firebase operations') with no mention that it is inert/refused in production. MISLEADING by omission given the prod 0-skew branch. Correct the header to dev/local-only.
- **llm_response.py:297-312 `get_narrative_text(debug_mode)` docstring** — Docstring: 'If True, include debug content. If False, strip debug content.' / 'Returns: Clean narrative text'. Code: `return self.narrative_text` unconditionally in both modes (comment in body: 'narrative_text is always clean'). The debug_mode parameter has NO effect. STALE — run-2 ADAPT confirmed; the docstring must be corrected to state the parameter is dead, or the parameter removed.
- **preventive_guards.py:1-70 module header** — Header mandates 'ALL safeguards MUST run on EVERY state update' and lists world-time, location, core-memory, resource-checkpoint, and DM-notes guards as required scope. Under run-2's confirmed policy verdicts those five guards are DELETE (see §4 live-code caveats); the header is the pre-audit defensive-layer claim, not the post-audit rule. STALE under current verdicts — header must be rewritten to the kept set (Social HP anti-blitz + server-owned-field stripping + god-mode extraction + async faction) if the DELETEs land.
- **main.py:1171-1185 `_strip_equipment_from_game_state` docstring** — Docstring says the bloat-campaign dedupe 'already shrinks equipment.backpack' yet stripping helps 'even BEFORE the inventory dedupe lands'. Both cannot describe the same present state; the sequencing ('already' vs 'before … lands') is contradictory/stale. (The function itself is DELETE-confirmed; the doc issue matters if the function survives any longer.)
- **llm_service.py:6480-6483 `_translate_completed_character_creation_finish_input` docstring** — Docstring: 'Translate the consumed CC finish button into a story-start action for the LLM.' Code: it acts ONLY when classifier_metadata routing_priority == '3_char_creation_complete', regex-strips two finish-marker patterns from user input, and injects a fixed story-start instruction ('The character creation modal has already been closed by the server…'). INCOMPLETE/MISLEADING by omission: the classifier-metadata gate and the injected instruction are the operative behaviour and are not described.
- **decorators.py module header** — Header promises 'Logger integration with emoji-enhanced logging'. The wrapper's actual error message (decorators.py log_exceptions.wrapper) contains no emoji and only function name, arg counts, kwarg keys, error, and traceback. MINOR STALE claim — cosmetic, but it is a doc/code mismatch.
- **Clarified, NOT stale (recorded so it is not re-flagged): llm_capture.py:56-101 `emit_llm_capture` honor_suppression** — The docstring and inline comments accurately state that honor_suppression 'currently has no effect on the BQ write' and only would gate a future duplicate generic echo. Run-2 correctly called the parameter a 'misleading control'; the documentation itself is accurate. The fix is to remove/rename the parameter, not to correct its docs.
- **Checked and accurate (not flagged):** arch.py docstrings ('counts that act as proxies for patterns', 'fake_patterns') honestly describe the lightweight counting; json_utils.py default_scoring comments accurately describe the +500 signals (the signals are the policy problem, not a doc problem); criterion4_admission.py read_admission_document docstring ('never infer from event data') matches its Firestore-authoritative implementation; lazy_module_loader.py header accurately describes the LazyLoader mechanism and the from-import caveat.

## (4) Challenges to run-2 verdicts with evidence

- **C1 — COUNTING (challenge to run-2's headline numbers).** Run-2 claims '593 def/class occurrences' covered by '586 table rows', and '26 files'. AST enumeration of the local tree gives: main 178, llm_service 152, mcp_api 35, mcp_client 29, llm_request 13, llm_response 29, llm_capture 3, continuation_dispatch 12, continuation_admission 16, client_platform 3, decorators 2, lazy_module_loader 1, gunicorn.conf 4, file_cache 4, clock_skew 9, circuit_breaker 4, custom_types 22, numeric_converters 2, numeric_field_converter 6, json_utils 4, arch 2, criterion4_admission 20, criterion4_correlation 12, input_validation 8, preventive_guards 22 — sum = **592, not 593**. Run-2's own per-file numbers sum to 592, so the 593 total is an arithmetic slip (off by one), and the grouped-row gap is 6 (592 − 586), not 7. Separately, run-2's '26 files' counts the JSON data file, but its file sections and line-count table name only the 25 Python files — criterion4_target_profiles.json has no run-2 row. This review closes that gap (§6/JSON row). No verdict changes follow from C1 alone; the ledger's 586-row verdict tally (KEEP 451 / ADAPT 97 / DELETE 38) is a row tally and must not be quoted as a symbol tally.
- **C2 — clock_skew patch family: DELETE → ADAPT (challenge).** Run-2 DELETEs: module state, CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS, _adjusted_utcnow, apply_clock_skew_patch, remove_clock_skew_patch, UseActualTime + __enter__/__exit__. Evidence against unconditional DELETE: world_logic.py:41 calls apply_clock_skew_patch() at import; openrouter_auth.py:234-235 calls get_clock_skew_seconds() and UseActualTime(); waitlist_access.py:57-59 does the same. In production those calls are no-ops (skew 0, _patch_applied False), so run-2's 'inert in production' premise is CONFIRMED — but inert-in-prod is not dead, and deleting the symbols breaks live non-prod code paths. Correct verdict: **ADAPT** — remove the patch from the production path (and its prod import in world_logic.py), retain as dev-only tooling or delete together with its non-prod callers in one PR. The 2026-10-29 deletion rule (non-use must be proven first) is not satisfied for this family. KEEP confirmed for _is_testing_mode and validate_deployment_config; ADAPT confirmed for get_clock_skew_seconds (plus its doc fix, §3).
- **C3 — llm_response._strip_debug_content: ADAPT → DELETE (challenge).** Run-2 kept it as a legacy shim. Evidence: zero prod callers (only llm_response.py:365 definition); the file's live legacy paths call _strip_all_debug_tags (llm_response.py:556,609), and the canonical strip_debug_content is imported directly by world_logic.py:102. No legacy caller for this wrapper was shown. DELETE (non-use proven by the grep). _strip_state_updates_only DELETE confirmed on the same evidence.
- **C4 — 'DELETE = dead' framing: QUALIFIED for all live policy-DELETEs (not a verdict reversal).** Run-2's DELETEs for coerce_integral_number (live: game_state.py:751,803; game_state_merge.py:3220+), preventive-guard backfills (live: preventive_guards.py:231-258; llm_parser.py:2862-2926), get_spell_level (live: main.py:4367,4399), _strip_equipment_from_game_state (live: main.py:2820), the llm_service strip/normalize family (live: llm_service.py:6611,6756,7762,8144,8217,8226,8348,8879,9195,10399,10916), and json_utils.default_scoring (live default: json_utils.py:150) are CONFIRMED as §4 policy verdicts, but each removal is a migration touching its live callers, not a dead-code sweep. Any PR description that calls these 'unused' would be false.
- **C5 — main.py nested calc_modifier: DELETE confirmed + duplication added.** Run-2 DELETE rested on gateway-location. Added evidence: stats_display.calc_modifier (stats_display.py:61) implements the same formula and get_stats already uses it (main.py:3972,3983,4018). The nested copy should be deleted in favour of the existing stats_display function, not merely 'moved out'.
- **C6 — Duplicated logic found by this review (name both locations):** (a) main.py get_stats.safe_get vs get_spells.safe_get (two nested copies of a safe dict read); (b) mcp_client.py call_tool_sync.run_in_fresh_loop vs get_resource_sync.run_in_fresh_loop (run-2 ADAPT confirmed — merge to one helper); (c) main.py + llm_service.py both define _is_mock_services_mode; (d) llm_service.py wrappers _materialize_continuation_dispatch and _emit_continuation_admission_telemetry duplicate continuation_dispatch.py / continuation_admission.py entry points (thin delegations — KEEP as seams, but the duplication is real and should not grow); (e) custom_types.py LLMRequest/LLMResponse TypedDicts vs llm_request.py LLMRequest dataclass vs llm_response.py LLMResponse class (three response/request shapes — run-2 noted the custom_types/llm_request distinction; the triple shape is the fuller duplication); (f) NumericFieldConverter.convert_dict (numeric_field_converter.py:117) is a verbatim alias for convert_all_possible_ints (run-2 DELETE confirmed for both); (g) criterion4_admission.py _task8_log_labels_match wrapper (criterion4_admission.py:587) duplicates task8_log_labels_match (run-2 DELETE confirmed); (h) llm_capture.py _coerce_str/_coerce_int duplicate generic coercion idioms also present in numeric_converters.py (not a verdict change — both are small and KEEP as local evidence-row helpers, but a future merge is available).
- **All other run-2 verdicts: CONFIRM.** In particular: main.py ADAPT (gateway + move embedded game logic out), llm_service.py ADAPT, mcp_api.py ADAPT (test dice tool out of prod list), mcp_client.py ADAPT, llm_response.py ADAPT (legacy strip family), continuation_admission.py ADAPT (delete _omit_redundant_game_state_narrative, continuation_admission.py:81), client_platform.py KEEP (App Store policy promise named), decorators.py ADAPT (async log delivery), lazy_module_loader.py KEEP, gunicorn.conf.py KEEP, file_cache.py KEEP, code_execution_circuit_breaker.py KEEP, custom_types.py ADAPT, numeric_field_converter.py ADAPT (blanket conversion DELETE for convert_all_possible_ints/convert_dict/convert_value), json_utils.py ADAPT, arch.py ADAPT, criterion4_admission.py KEEP (except the wrapper DELETE), criterion4_correlation.py KEEP, input_validation.py KEEP (except sanitize_user_input ADAPT), preventive_guards.py ADAPT (Social HP KEEP; faction ADAPT to async per §6(3); backfills DELETE). No §6 conflict was found in this group beyond the clock-skew qualification in C2 (which is a prod-path placement issue, not a §6(1)/(2)/(3) deletion).

## (5) Draft-amendment effects

The draft at ~/workspace/plans/zfc-skill-model-amendment-proposal.md ('FastEmbed or Jev count as models') is DRAFT ONLY and was NOT applied as policy. Verdicts above are under current §4. Where the draft would change reasoning, it is flagged here — in no case does it change a verdict in this group:

- **llm_service.py `_translate_completed_character_creation_finish_input` (DELETE): NO CHANGE.** Even if the classifier metadata it keys on counted as model-authored output, the function still applies hand-written regex stripping of finish markers and injects a fixed server instruction. Under the draft's own text, 'hand-written domain rules layered on a model output' remain field-aware and need an exception. No exception is named. Verdict stays DELETE.
- **llm_service.py `_normalize_pre_agent_cc_flags` (DELETE): NO CHANGE.** This rewrites persisted/model flags before agent selection; it is not dispatch on a Jev Choice or an embedding similarity, so the draft does not touch it. Verdict stays DELETE.
- **FastEmbed in this group: NO EFFECT.** The only FastEmbed mentions in the 25 Python files are comments (main.py:46,1545; gunicorn.conf.py:57, about warmup/cold reload). No FastEmbed call, threshold, boost, or anchor list exists in this group's code, so the draft's FastEmbed clause has nothing to re-judge here.
- **Jev in this group: NO EFFECT.** No Jev call, Choice, Noul, or Score handling exists in these 25 files. The §6(4) Jev routing decision (dispatch table server-side, explicit-signals-only conclude guards, Noul flags for confirmation only) does not alter any gateway verdict; it confirms the direction of the _translate DELETE (finish must be an explicit signal, not a translated classifier inference).
- **json_utils.py `default_scoring` (+500 for narrative/god_mode_response): NO EFFECT.** This is server-side candidate selection over parsed JSON, not dispatch on a model decision output under the draft's definition. Verdict stays DELETE.
- **All other rows: NO DRAFT EFFECT.** No other symbol in this group dispatches on an embedding similarity or a Jev decision; the draft therefore changes reasoning for zero additional rows here.
